Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any quantum module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 969. The projective resolution has length at most 759 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to quantum structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 969/759 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the quantum framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 273. The resulting operator achieves bound O(697/n^3).
